# B0247 — Recognition, performance and shared work

These three Event families make shared performance productive without making everyone mean the same thing by it. KARAKURI's encounter with Nanasta yields reciprocal recognition and public confusion while Haru's experience remains incompletely explained. Ferb and Yumeno prepare and retrospectively enjoy a live through markedly different idioms of attachment; Ferb's explicit musical appreciation and objections delimit the romantic meanings Yumeno supplies. At the school festival, 4U's publicly promised cooperation becomes differentiated work, disputed authority and retrospective accounts, before Hina names a celebration for the three bandmates. The common concern is how an occasion can sustain a relationship while leaving motives, interpretation and obligation unequal. The families supply different answers, not one cure for their members' older conflicts.

#### Interpretation and bounded alternatives

**CL-B0247-001 — The first chapter organizes a crisis of classification before offering any causal solution**

The first chapter organizes a crisis of classification before offering any causal solution. Hime can call KARAKURI not-idols while remembering their song; the twins in turn treat Nanasta idol/Sister naming as an object of curiosity. Recognition is therefore socially positioned rather than one neutral taxonomy shared by everyone. The opening does not justify replacing either group with a new universal category.

**CL-B0247-006 — Haru utterances and the pale-haired alternate resource are directly supplied, but the mechanism is not**

Haru utterances and the pale-haired alternate resource are directly supplied, but the mechanism is not. Musubi absorption hypothesis, a comic assimilation/enactment reading and the twins stated surprise must remain distinguishable. The current chapter does not decide intention, actual telepathy, neurological cause, a disguise process or a changed legal/person identity. Its ending distributes uncertainty to Player/Hime, rescue effort to Musubi and enjoyment to Coney rather than supplying one shared interpretation.

Coney enjoyment is source-local. Her last joke does not prove she arranged the incident, knows its mechanism or will repair it. Likewise the unresolved ending does not cancel Haru already published broader states; this is a separate Event interval whose exact placement relative to earlier Main, Intimacy and Birthday material is not supplied. #### Interpretation and bounded alternatives

**CL-B0247-008 — The collaboration converts different motives into a shared stage without first resolving the participants explanation of Haru**

The collaboration converts different motives into a shared stage without first resolving the participants explanation of Haru. Player sees a scarce opportunity, Musubi sees a rescue, Hime a competitive test, Coney an entertainment and training stimulus, and the twins an interesting expanded performance. The stage can therefore produce cooperation without a common account of what the cooperation is doing. Haru/Mitsuba stated commitment is present; it must neither be erased into total passivity nor inflated into a complete informed-assent or recollection account.

#### Directional relationships in this interval

- **Player → KARAKURI and Nanasta performers:** Accepts bounded collaboration and proposes rival growth; opportunity does not erase anxiety or merge institutions. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0168.json__0f1b79cfdc8b90aa?doc=primary&log=7#/Pages/7`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0168.json__0f1b79cfdc8b90aa?doc=primary&log=8#/Pages/8`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0168.json__0f1b79cfdc8b90aa?doc=primary&log=32#/Pages/32`.

- **Hitoha → Nanasta performers:** Renews recognition and allows a qualified visit; rivalry and limits remain. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0168.json__0f1b79cfdc8b90aa?doc=primary&log=29#/Pages/29`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0168.json__0f1b79cfdc8b90aa?doc=primary&log=35#/Pages/35`.
- **Futaba → Nanasta performers:** Values a stage beyond the pair and Nanasta's different world; her revision is not Hitoha's whole private state. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0168.json__0f1b79cfdc8b90aa?doc=primary&log=25#/Pages/25`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0168.json__0f1b79cfdc8b90aa?doc=primary&log=26#/Pages/26`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0168.json__0f1b79cfdc8b90aa?doc=primary&log=30#/Pages/30`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0168.json__0f1b79cfdc8b90aa?doc=primary&log=36#/Pages/36`.

Ferb twice clarifies that she means Yumeno costume. Yumeno continues considering Ferb looks and returns to a wedding dress, while Player again distinguishes live from wedding. The misunderstanding partly belongs to Player expectations about whose appearance Ferb would consider. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0170.json__8ae447aaca975835?doc=primary&log=7#/Pages/7`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0170.json__8ae447aaca975835?doc=primary&log=18#/Pages/18`.
- P19-27: Ferb proposes a silhouette that expresses Yumeno femininity/cuteness. Yumeno asks whether Ferb called her cute; Ferb affirms and asks what is odd about it. Yumeno names the day a love-confession anniversary and requests celebratory red rice. Player calls this an interpretive leap. Ferb asks Yumeno to try a candidate; Yumeno says her own outfit can be casual because she has received Ferb feelings. Ferb refuses that dismissal and invokes Yumeno own date framing as a reason to dress properly. Yumeno responds with extravagant happiness and a death-tomorrow idiom. No actual confession agreement, meal or prediction of harm is established. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0170.json__8ae447aaca975835?doc=primary&log=19#/Pages/19`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0170.json__8ae447aaca975835?doc=primary&log=27#/Pages/27`.
- P28-35: A PLAYER-labelled line says the candidate suits Yumeno; its native Sound names cue170037, which lacks a canonical audio-registry binding. The written attribution is retained. Ferb asks whether Player agrees and proposes matching her own outfit to Yumeno; Player endorses a shared concept. Yumeno repeats matching/pair-look/date associations. Ferb declares costumes decided and turns to song choice, then urges Yumeno onward while she continues written laughter. The matching concept is chosen by speech; its exact design, actual wearing and song remain undisclosed. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0170.json__8ae447aaca975835?doc=primary&log=28#/Pages/28`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0170.json__8ae447aaca975835?doc=primary&log=35#/Pages/35`.
- P36-38: The pair is faded/cleared in native instructions. Player calls Yumeno overheated, wonders whether Ferb might also be interested and notes that her attention outside music seems unusual. He then offers another possibility: because costumes preceded music, perhaps she only wanted to finish quickly. Both are his questions, not an authoritative disclosure of Ferb motive or an actual medical observation. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0170.json__8ae447aaca975835?doc=primary&log=36#/Pages/36`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0170.json__8ae447aaca975835?doc=primary&log=38#/Pages/38`.

#### Interpretation and bounded alternatives

**CL-B0247-024 — Ferb begins by minimizing clothing as a demand on herself, yet she actively considers Yumeno appearance**

Ferb begins by minimizing clothing as a demand on herself, yet she actively considers Yumeno appearance. That is a meaningful shift of attention, not merely passive tolerance of another person fantasy. Her corrections to Player make the recipient of her concern explicit. The chapter adds a specific act of presentational care while retaining her stated musical priority.

**CL-B0247-025 — The comedy depends on several people misreading the object of an utterance**

The comedy depends on several people misreading the object of an utterance. Player hears Ferb question as self-styling, then as asking how to handle Yumeno. Yumeno hears an aesthetic appraisal as a love confession. Ferb has to repeat whom she is discussing, but her own reuse of date vocabulary also gives Yumeno more material for the romantic frame. No one neutral observer resolves all of these semantic crossings.

**CL-B0247-026 — Yumeno is both a source of objectifying attention and a recipient of valued recognition here**

Yumeno is both a source of objectifying attention and a recipient of valued recognition here. Her interest in Ferb clothes/body is authored; so is her surprise that Ferb calls her cute. This expands the local account beyond a one-way gaze without erasing earlier boundary failures or treating affirmation as a complete reform. Her confession-anniversary interpretation remains hers, while Ferb affirmative response to the cuteness question remains a real positive utterance.

**CL-B0247-027 — The date metaphor now acquires practical force**

The date metaphor now acquires practical force. Ferb uses Yumeno own term to insist that she attend to her own appearance, then elects to match her costume to the selected partner concept. That is reciprocal participation in a presentational project. It need not be flattened into either a confirmed romance or a meaningless accidental word choice; the source leaves the scope and motive open.

**CL-B0247-028 — The chosen matching concept is an actual local result, whereas specific garments and later wearing are not shown**

The chosen matching concept is an actual local result, whereas specific garments and later wearing are not shown. The stills deliberately retain ordinary body/outfit resources even as the conversation selects stage clothes. Distinguish a decision reported by a participant from a visible finished costume, and distinguish proposed wedding styling from the coordinated live concept that Ferb declares settled.

**CL-B0247-029 — Player ending preserves interpretive alternatives**

Player ending preserves interpretive alternatives. He first recognizes attention that complicates his music-only type description, then proposes practical haste as another explanation. The final question does not cancel the preceding positive attention or certify its private cause. Ferb own later account, if supplied, will be stronger evidence than choosing one observer hypothesis now.

**CL-B0247-030 — Written laughter, the death-tomorrow idiom, Player overheated shorthand and decorative hearts/spiral face features belong to the comic register**

Written laughter, the death-tomorrow idiom, Player overheated shorthand and decorative hearts/spiral face features belong to the comic register. They support analysis of exaggerated response and the scene focus, not a diagnosis, actual threat, performed vocal delivery or a new body-state fact. The unresolved Player-labelled audio reference likewise does not authorize silently assigning his appraisal to Yumeno or Ferb.

#### Interpretation and bounded alternatives

**CL-B0247-031 — Song selection exposes a practical problem beneath the flirtation comedy**

Song selection exposes a practical problem beneath the flirtation comedy. A broad near-future brief does not give Ferb a usable musical direction, and Player's seasonal suggestion draws out how little either woman has experienced the festival as an ordinary visitor. The narrative moves from a proposed image to the personal histories that make that image difficult to inhabit; it does not immediately produce a song.

**CL-B0247-032 — The two accounts are parallel without being interchangeable**

The two accounts are parallel without being interchangeable. Yumeno's creative labor and deadlines consume the period before the festival; Ferb's concentration on performing consumes the festival itself. Both are involved in cultural production, yet neither recalls much of the general visitor experience. This gives substance to the earlier music-centered description of Ferb while also giving Yumeno an activity and cost beyond her romantic fantasies.

**CL-B0247-033 — Yumeno's claim not to remember is explained locally through exhaustion, sleeping and attention directed at a couple**

Yumeno's claim not to remember is explained locally through exhaustion, sleeping and attention directed at a couple. It is not a diagnosis of amnesia. Her account of following the couple also preserves the issue of boundaries around other people's intimacy. The chapter can make her wish to join that shared experience intelligible without treating the reported following as reciprocal consent or making her attraction itself the ethical problem.

**CL-B0247-034 — Ferb neither answers the full takoyaki/haunted-house proposal nor ends the conversation**

Ferb neither answers the full takoyaki/haunted-house proposal nor ends the conversation. Her topic transition can support Player's observation that she passes over Yumeno's request, but his admiring gloss is still an observer's interpretation. The subsequent account of music, noise and distant enjoyment supplies her own preferences. She is selective about ways of participating, rather than reducible to an unfeeling counterpart to an excitable admirer.

**CL-B0247-035 — The eventual coffee agreement is narrower than Yumeno's date fantasy and more positive than a simple rejection**

The eventual coffee agreement is narrower than Yumeno's date fantasy and more positive than a simple rejection. Ferb states a limit around sweets, offers an alternative she can share and asks Yumeno to prepare. This is a small negotiated form of companionship. It preserves each person's different preferences and makes the relationship readable through specific accommodations instead of requiring an immediate romantic label.

Ferb also retains agency over the work: she asks for a brief, identifies insufficient direction, requests time and proposes a concrete next activity. Player permits the deferral rather than coercing an instant decision. The instrument trip and offered coffee can coexist with the unresolved motive questions from Chapter 2; neither practical purpose nor social willingness cancels the other.

**CL-B0247-037 — Player's closing question registers that closeness may appear without the clinginess Yumeno imagines**

Player's closing question registers that closeness may appear without the clinginess Yumeno imagines. It does not settle Ferb's private feeling or the whole relationship. His forgotten budget warning then restores a managerial blind spot beneath his interpretive commentary. The possibility of an over-budget purchase is a prospective concern, not a completed event that can be added to the factual ledger.

#### Interpretation and bounded alternatives

**CL-B0247-038 — The instrument choices extend Chapter 2's reciprocal attention into a concrete working decision**

The instrument choices extend Chapter 2's reciprocal attention into a concrete working decision. Yumeno chooses by how an instrument looks with Ferb; Ferb chooses for Yumeno after asking about her way of making sound. Neither simply copies the other's criterion. #### Interpretation and bounded alternatives

**CL-B0247-046 — The ending changes the available interpretation through Ferb own clarification**

The ending changes the available interpretation through Ferb own clarification. Her earlier unusual attention did not by itself disclose a private motive; now she names a specific positive attachment to Yumeno distinctive sound and explicitly confirms its musical scope. That clarification resolves the meaning of this utterance without invalidating the genuine presentational care, coffee offer or instrumental attention already shown. It also supplies no basis to infer every other possible feeling.

Evidence: `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0173.json__94129cd0fa928804?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0173.json__94129cd0fa928804?doc=primary&log=14#/Pages/14`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0173.json__94129cd0fa928804?doc=primary&log=15#/Pages/15`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0173.json__94129cd0fa928804?doc=primary&log=27#/Pages/27`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0173.json__94129cd0fa928804?doc=primary&log=28#/Pages/28`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0173.json__94129cd0fa928804?doc=primary&log=37#/Pages/37`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0173.json__94129cd0fa928804?doc=primary&log=38#/Pages/38`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0173.json__94129cd0fa928804?doc=primary&log=44#/Pages/44`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0173.json__94129cd0fa928804?doc=primary&log=45#/Pages/45`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0173.json__94129cd0fa928804?doc=primary&log=49#/Pages/49`.

**CL-B0247-047 — Musical recognition is the substantive result**

Musical recognition is the substantive result. Ferb wants to repeat the collaboration because she values something she regards as uniquely Yumeno's. The emotional difference Player identifies can therefore become a resource in playing together instead of evidence that two temperaments are incompatible. Yet his explanation remains a participant's synthesis, and the operator has not heard the groove, accents or distinctive sound described by the performers.

**CL-B0247-048 — The omitted live matters to the form of the evidence**

The omitted live matters to the form of the evidence. The chapter presents an aftermath, not the process by which a song was chosen or the stage coordination achieved. Ferb, Yumeno and Player each report different aspects of the event: musical pleasure, approach/attention and audience response. Their reports support a successful shared occasion as narrated, while withholding set list, precise arrangement, actual coordinated costumes and independently inspected technical quality.

**CL-B0247-049 — The ambiguity of like gives the comedy a real epistemic asymmetry**

The ambiguity of like gives the comedy a real epistemic asymmetry. Ferb specifies music in answer to Player, while Yumeno continues to speak as if her desired relationship has been established. No explicit uptake of the correction is shown. Player considers preserving the pleasing fantasy rather than making its limits the center of a confrontation. The analysis should not adopt either the fantasy as a mutual agreement or Player's amusement as proof that the misunderstanding has no cost.

**CL-B0247-050 — Yumeno's wife claim and invoked wedding vow meet a direct denial and an uncertain question, respectively**

Yumeno's wife claim and invoked wedding vow meet a direct denial and an uncertain question, respectively. These exchanges keep the earlier semantically crossed conversation active; an imagined aisle has not become a shared promise through repetition. Ferb's explicit no-hug objection in Chapter 4 also retains full force. Musical warmth and willingness to repeat a live do not retroactively consent to that embrace or establish unrestricted access.

**CL-B0247-051 — The ice-bassist nickname shifts the vulnerability back to Ferb**

The ice-bassist nickname shifts the vulnerability back to Ferb. A performer who has just shown unusual confidence and enthusiasm balks at a label she considers too presumptuous. Player relates it to her admiration for Mito. The contrast allows a musician to be both an evaluator of another person's sound and an admirer who feels small before an exemplar. It does not require a new Mito appearance, a ranking of their technique or access to either person's whole private life.

**CL-B0247-052 — The close retains difference rather than curing it**

The close retains difference rather than curing it. Ferb returns repeatedly to the next performance, while Yumeno keeps converting shared activity into imagined intimacy. They can want another occasion together for partly different reasons. The specific result is a renewed musical invitation and recognition; no permanent unit, approved couple, complete boundary repair or universal relationship harmony is supplied.

Written excitement, laughter, idioms and face changes are sufficient for this text/static reading. The chapter can complete the local event while leaving those modality and downstream practical limits explicit.

#### Causal structure

- The institutional live proposal creates a task with practical decisions. Choosing clothes, looking for a song and shopping become occasions for learning what the other values. Ferb ability to take Yumeno appearance and musical commitment seriously provides positive content before the final clarification; the story is not simply a sequence of unwanted advances receiving no response.
- Several comic misunderstandings change the object of a statement: musical walking becomes an imagined aisle, an aesthetic judgment becomes a confession, a fictional senior is temporarily heard as a real acquaintance, and like initially sounds broader than Ferb intended. Correction is uneven. Player sometimes clarifies and sometimes misreads; Yumeno often retains a desired frame. This repeated structure is a narrative mechanism, not proof of a clinical disorder or unlimited ignorance.
- Two instrumental origins give the pair a shared structure of admiration without making their models the same kind of person. The fictional model yields real purchase and practice in Yumeno account; Ferb chooses complementarity as a way to imagine playing with Mito. The distinction between copying, joining and creating becomes concrete in their present reciprocal choices. Mito own feelings remain outside the scene.
- The live is a causal bridge in the narrated sequence, but its process is elided. Ferb says she realized today how much she likes Yumeno distinctive sound; this supplies a stated basis for asking to work together again. It does not retrospectively disclose every motive in Chapters 1–4, independently verify acoustic qualities or reveal the missing song-selection process.
- Positive partnership and physical limits coexist. Ferb local okay, coordinated-costume proposal, coffee alternative, instrumental care and later invitation do not cancel her no-hug objection or wife denial. The story ends with a specific form of musical compatibility while leaving mismatched expectations active. No general cure, consent waiver, permanent group or romance/no-romance verdict follows.
- Managerial narration is itself fallible. Player recognizes a useful complementary difference by the end, but earlier he misassigns selection criteria, initially accepts the senior anecdote as ordinary biography, forgets a budget warning and considers letting Yumeno retain a fantasy. His final harmony interpretation must be assessed alongside these blind spots and the participants' own words.

Ume begins with studio/rehearsal plans and still allows a crepe stop, so priorities and discretion are present, but neither fact establishes an unlimited amount of free time. The source shows her performing regret and invoking band work. It leaves the actual schedule and amount of effort open.

**CL-B0247-060 — Emoco uses a new institutional role to reverse the direction of demands**

Emoco uses a new institutional role to reverse the direction of demands. Her explanation that ordering is preferable to being ordered fits the bandmates' reported familiarity with her preferences. It does not make every school duty an illusion or prove that she has no work. The tension is that she speaks for the band's cooperation and offers the others' labor before distinguishing her own committee burden.

**CL-B0247-061 — Hina tries to turn the arrangement into an explicitly shared three-person effort**

Hina tries to turn the arrangement into an explicitly shared three-person effort. Her enthusiasm is not only passive mediation or an appetite gag: she accepts the class connection and formulates a joint plan. Emoco's exclusion then exposes the difference between a collective label and a negotiated distribution of tasks. Ume's protest and Hina's final dismay keep that distribution contested.

Nevertheless, their A/B/collective labels remain anonymous local roles. A shared generic silhouette cannot merge them into a named individual or furnish personal histories.

**CL-B0247-063 — Private parentheses separate related but nonidentical knowledge**

Private parentheses separate related but nonidentical knowledge. Hina appraises Ume's register change and Emoco's aversion to commands; Ume remembers a particular teacher argument. These thoughts are available to the reader at their named pages, not automatically exchanged between the characters. Emoco's own preference statement can be recorded without converting either recollection into an omniscient account of her entire motive.

**CL-B0247-064 — The chapter's full-cooperation premise is deliberately unstable**

The chapter's full-cooperation premise is deliberately unstable. A band can be publicly committed while its members disagree about authority, timing and who will do the work. The title's question/exclamation register is borne out by the scene; no complete social reform, permanent school leadership, resolved group governance or finished festival success is available at this first-chapter horizon.

- P0-5: In the mall-background frame, Ume says she accepted the earlier assignment because it was in front of everyone but remains unconvinced. This supplies her own retrospective account of reluctant public acceptance at this chapter's horizon. Emoco dismisses her complaint as habitual yapping. Ume insists it is an objection to being ordered and asks Hina to support her; Hina answers with appreciation for crepes rather than the authority dispute. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0175.json__a70d96c3ff0a4638?doc=primary&log=0#/Pages/0`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0175.json__a70d96c3ff0a4638?doc=primary&log=5#/Pages/5`.
- P6-16: Hina describes batter on a griddle, the T-shaped tombo spreader, thin even batter, smell and the timing of a palette knife. Ume asks what tombo means; Emoco does not supply an answer. Hina calls the multisensory process a kind of total art and answers Emoco cheap-art remark by saying art is limitless. This is an articulated appreciation and procedural description, not an actually demonstrated cooking performance. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0175.json__a70d96c3ff0a4638?doc=primary&log=6#/Pages/6`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0175.json__a70d96c3ff0a4638?doc=primary&log=16#/Pages/16`.
- P17-23: Emoco says she had not considered crepes worth that much thought and describes a kind of respect for such enthusiasm, then qualifies it as respect for expending effort on something she calls useless. Hina initially thanks her for understanding and then treats the qualification as a wish to care about toppings too. Emoco has an exact written ellipsis at P22, whose native cue175062 is unavailable in the canonical registry. Ume enjoys seeing what she calls Emoco speechless face. No literal acoustic silence or settled private respect is established. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0175.json__a70d96c3ff0a4638?doc=primary&log=17#/Pages/17`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0175.json__a70d96c3ff0a4638?doc=primary&log=23#/Pages/23`.
- P24-29: Hina lists preferred fruit, chocolate and vanilla ice cream; Ume adds butter-sugar as her own favorite. Emoco calls Ume choice cheap and Ume challenges that appraisal. Hina responds about plentiful whipped cream, prompting Ume to note that cream was not part of that exchange. The conversation presents preferences and mismatched replies, not a class-approved menu, actual prices, prepared food or purchases. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0175.json__a70d96c3ff0a4638?doc=primary&log=24#/Pages/24`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0175.json__a70d96c3ff0a4638?doc=primary&log=29#/Pages/29`.
- P30-39: Hina anticipates eating many crepes at the festival. Ume corrects her: they are to make crepes for sale at the stall rather than primarily for their own eating. Hina expresses disappointment. Emoco uses a chick/feeding metaphor; Hina denies that reaction by saying an open mouth would let drool out. Ume and Emoco have a joint-labelled written ellipsis, and Hina says disappointment has made her hungry. No actual drooling, deprivation rule, completed stall work or resolved division of labor is shown before the final fade. `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0175.json__a70d96c3ff0a4638?doc=primary&log=30#/Pages/30`; `t7s://c20260909-r484/v1/script/memory_0175.json__a70d96c3ff0a4638?doc=primary&log=39#/Pages/39`.

#### Interpretation and bounded alternatives

**CL-B0247-065 — The chapter confirms that public acceptance did not amount to private agreement**

The chapter confirms that public acceptance did not amount to private agreement. Ume now says directly why she yielded in front of the classmates and renews the authority complaint. That new account belongs to this chapter rather than being inserted into the earlier freeze. The conflict is then displaced by Hina's interest, leaving the original issue of who may promise another person's labor unresolved.

**CL-B0247-066 — Hina's appetite includes a specific way of attending to craft**

Hina's appetite includes a specific way of attending to craft. She notices a tool, hand movement, thickness, smell and a moment of judgment, giving her enthusiasm a procedural and sensory shape. Ume and Emoco lack the term she supplies in this exchange. This is meaningful ordinary-life characterization without proof that Hina herself can reproduce every technique or a reason to promote a complete culinary-expertise model.

**CL-B0247-067 — Her claim that art is limitless unsettles Emoco's price-based dismissal**

Her claim that art is limitless unsettles Emoco's price-based dismissal. Emoco first grants an almost irresistible force to the utterance, then qualifies her apparent respect through another insult. The sequence gives Hina's conviction a local power without requiring Emoco to undergo an unspoken conversion. It also lets an everyday inexpensive pleasure carry an aesthetic seriousness that her bandmates do not initially share.

**CL-B0247-068 — The failure of the insult to produce the expected answer is structurally important**

The failure of the insult to produce the expected answer is structurally important. Hina converts Emoco's qualification into a toppings proposal and continues elaborating. Whether this is absorption in her own image, a selective positive reading or a deliberate deflection is not resolved by private narration. The authored mismatch is clear; its full psychological cause is not. Ume can enjoy the interruption without the analyst asserting that Emoco has lost the physical ability to speak.

**CL-B0247-069 — Roles briefly realign**

Roles briefly realign. Ume sought Hina as an ally against Emoco, but Hina does not answer that request. Ume then joins the food-preference exchange and enjoys Emoco's written pause, while Ume and Emoco later share a joint ellipsis at Hina's drooling conditional. These local alignments make the trio more dynamic than fixed controller, rebel and peacekeeper positions.

**CL-B0247-070 — The turn from eating to selling returns material labor to the fantasy**

The turn from eating to selling returns material labor to the fantasy. Hina's detailed appreciation of the finished experience did not yet include the role assigned to her as a producer. Ume supplies that role distinction. It is a correction about what running this stall involves, not independent proof that volunteers can never eat anything at the festival or that Hina is actually being deprived of food.

**CL-B0247-071 — The discussion also limits claims about the proposed menu and economic value**

The discussion also limits claims about the proposed menu and economic value. Toppings, butter-sugar and cream are personal preferences in conversation; Emoco's cheapness remark is an evaluative jab. There is no voted menu, budget, price list or prepared inventory. These statements can support characterization of taste, hierarchy and sensory attention without being converted into unobserved administrative facts.

**CL-B0247-072 — The ending retains both Hina's assertive enthusiasm and her disappointment**

The ending retains both Hina's assertive enthusiasm and her disappointment. The chick metaphor is Emoco's teasing language and the drool line is a conditional joke, not a biological or clinical fact. No amount of comic charm resolves the earlier uneven commitment; that practical question remains available for the next chapter.

#### Interpretation and bounded alternatives

**CL-B0247-081 — The finale presents the festival through an aftermath that redistributes credit and embarrassment**

The finale presents the festival through an aftermath that redistributes credit and embarrassment. Hina's consumption, initially treated as a failure to understand production, is now also credited by Ume with attracting customers. That does not prove a quantified advertising result, but it complicates the earlier simple opposition between eating and contributing. Pleasure can have an unintended public use, and Emoco can recognize the image effect while retaining a cynical vocabulary.

**CL-B0247-082 — Ume's theatrical promise has become a reported Juliet performance**

Ume's theatrical promise has become a reported Juliet performance. The new confirmation belongs to this chapter, while the earlier casting uncertainty remains correctly frozen. Her claim that talent can do anything sits beside accounts of script study, stress, fatigue and disputed tears. The evidence supports real labor behind the public confidence without requiring every mocking account to be accepted at face value.

**CL-B0247-083 — The sleep joke turns acting into a problem of interpretation**

The sleep joke turns acting into a problem of interpretation. Hina can be moved by a scene, Emoco can laugh and later accuse Ume of sleeping, and Ume can recast a suspiciously prolonged stillness as direction. The near-admission about fatigue gives the accidental-sleep interpretation substantial comic force, but her explicit denial remains part of the source. The analyst may favor that reading as an inference while keeping the event record attributed and the reported five-minute duration approximate.

**CL-B0247-084 — Reception is plural**

Reception is plural. Hina's pleasure and reported tears coexist with Emoco's laughter and account of an audience stir. None independently supplies an objective technical score or the response of every spectator. Ume's repeated Romeo line gives a small present demonstration in written dialogue, not recovery of the omitted complete theatrical act or a separately heard performance.

**CL-B0247-085 — Emoco's account of chairmanship also becomes less one-dimensional**

Emoco's account of chairmanship also becomes less one-dimensional. She assumes that authority should withdraw so others can relax, yet Hina reports that colleagues welcomed her participation. Her own description of delegating paperwork through looks and glasses preserves the instrumental/abrasive idiom. The scene permits a local positive social reception without proving universal affection, fair labor allocation or a hidden wholly benevolent motive.

**CL-B0247-086 — The last question reveals shared time that was not dictated by the institutional tasks**

The last question reveals shared time that was not dictated by the institutional tasks. Ume and Hina have stayed after their own groups dispersed. Ume explains this through compensation and payment, while Hina names a three-member 4U celebration. Waiting can function as relational availability without being reduced either to a pure altruistic confession or to an invoice with no attachment. The cost claim and the wish to be together are both present.

**CL-B0247-087 — Food keeps changing function across the sequence: optional detour, craft, imagined consumption, saleable product, disputed tasting, stress accompaniment, public image and finally a proposed communal meal**

Food keeps changing function across the sequence: optional detour, craft, imagined consumption, saleable product, disputed tasting, stress accompaniment, public image and finally a proposed communal meal. These functions are attributed to particular speakers and situations. They enrich ordinary-life characterization without supplying a medical account of hunger, stress eating or body size, nor a measured financial model.

**CL-B0247-088 — The ending returns the three from separate school roles to a band-defined occasion while preserving their different registers**

The ending returns the three from separate school roles to a band-defined occasion while preserving their different registers. Hina invites, Ume demands recompense and Emoco protests excess celebration; the joint shock at more food adds another temporary alignment. Affiliation and friction coexist. This is a bounded enactment of company after uneven work, not proof that authority disputes, earlier care/complicity or wider grievances have been permanently solved.

### Complete-family causal and epistemic reconstruction

#### Factual reconstruction

- Chapter 1 begins with Ume studio/rehearsal plans and Hina crepe-shop interest. Anonymous classmates ask them to help their festival stall. Ume performs a regretful public refusal; Emoco interrupts, identifies herself as festival executive-committee chair and commits the band to full cooperation. Ume objects, Hina proposes all three working together, and Emoco says her committee workload prevents her direct stall help.
- Chapter 2 supplies Ume own account of having reluctantly accepted in front of the class. Her request for Hina support is displaced by Hina detailed craft/sensory appreciation of crepes, which redirects and briefly interrupts Emoco disparaging replies. Personal tastes are discussed before Ume corrects imagined eating into the producer/seller role. The labor dispute remains unresolved.
- Chapter 3 reports ongoing preparation and identifies Ume intended day-of-sales/attraction role. Her attempt to limit preparatory work leads classmate C to propose drama-club participation. Public confidence and private literary uncertainty diverge around Shakespeare and Lear. Hina offers Romeo and Juliet; Ume embraces the idea and promises a perfect Juliet, while actual exact casting and performance remain unconfirmed at that chapter freeze. The trio ends with differentiated tasks and continuing friction.
- Chapter 4 supplies the completed festival aftermath and confirms Ume Juliet performance through the participants. Consumption, customer appeal, rehearsal difficulty, tears, laughter, an unusually prolonged death scene and possible sleep receive differing accounts. Hina reports colleagues welcomed Emoco at the committee party; Emoco recounts convenient delegation. The others have waited after their own groups dispersed. Ume asks for compensatory celebration at Emoco expense and Hina calls it a three-member 4U party. No further meal or payment is shown.

#### Causal structure

- The school request creates a practical conflict among band time, class contribution and committee authority. Emoco role gives her a way to issue the demands she dislikes receiving, while the collective 4U label does not establish agreement about who owes which work. Ume public mask shapes how commitments are accepted before classmates; Hina attempts to give the commitment a shared form.
- Hina appetite also becomes a mode of attention. Her account of tools, timing, texture and smell gives a cheap everyday object aesthetic seriousness. Her replies do not fit Emoco intended insult and briefly reverse the control of conversation. This is a local effect of authored mismatched meanings, not proof of a deliberate psychological technique or a permanent cognitive trait.
- Ume concern with the valuable public role creates a second task. Persuading C to seek a more accessible play makes apparent expertise produce a request for an alternative. Hina supplies that alternative. The classroom performance of knowledge thus becomes an obligation to study and act; the aftermath then reports real labor behind the earlier bluff.
- The festival is elided rather than directly staged. Its results arrive through a retrospective conversation in which credit, embarrassment and evaluation are contested. Hina eating is both criticized as excessive and credited with customer appeal. Juliet is both praised and mocked. A plausible accidental-sleep joke coexists with Ume explicit denial. These different reception layers cannot be flattened into perfect success, total failure or a technical score.
- Committee authority also meets counterevidence to Emoco expectation. She assumes a chair would inhibit the party, while Hina reports welcome. Her own paper-filing account keeps an instrumental/abrasive idiom. The source supports local social reception and ongoing command habits together without establishing that all delegation was fair or that contempt secretly meant pure care.
- The last waiting period creates company outside the assigned school roles. Ume frames it through compensation for unequal trouble and Hina through a band-defined occasion. Their continued availability can be read as relational action without choosing a sole altruistic or economic motive. Friction remains part of the idiom; ending together does not retroactively justify every imposition.

#### Epistemic boundaries

- Known-then transitions are retained: Chapter1 has a contested public commitment; Chapter2 adds Ume own reluctant-acceptance account; Chapter3 has a play proposal and Ume Juliet promise; Chapter4 adds explicit Juliet/performance confirmation. Later disclosure does not rewrite earlier uncertainty or stage an unshown rehearsal.
- Private parentheses have named owners. Hina ninja/command-aversion appraisals and Ume teacher recollection or literary uncertainty are available to the reader at the specific pages; voice metadata does not make them automatically shared knowledge. Unnamed classmates A/B/C, the collective, teacher and committee participants retain bounded local roles.
- All final reception/economic accounts remain attributed. Hina disputes Emoco consumption amount; near-loss is not completed deficit; Ume customer-attraction account is not a quantified causal measurement. Hina tears/appraisal, Emoco laughter/stir account and Ume self-praise coexist without an audience census or independent performance review.
- The source strongly invites an accidental-sleep interpretation through Emoco reported near-five-minute pause and Ume fatigue explanation, followed by Hina question and Ume denial. That is a warranted comic inference with counterstatement, not an unqualified externally confirmed event, clinical condition or operator-measured timing.
- Emoco committee office, workload, colleague welcome and paper delegation have different reporters and evidential scopes. Election history, exact timetable, complete task totals and colleagues private motives remain unknown. Glasses gestures are reported, not rendered in the current body assets.
- The family is a school-event interval within the admitted 2034 program. Its internal preparation-to-aftermath progression is explicit, but no exact calendar placement relative to Main, other Events or later-life states follows from catalog order. Named Shakespeare works are cultural references inside this source, not additional source-work admission.

Her final demand for repayment and decision to wait coexist. #### Literary synthesis with retained alternatives

**CL-B0247-089 — The promise of full cooperation exposes contested authority and divided work.**

Emoco's committee-chair role supplies a school-specific institutional position; her claim to command inferiors is contested by Ume and does not establish band leadership. Offering Ume and Hina's labor while citing her own committee duties makes collective cooperation unequal from the start. Later Hina's crepe preparation, Emoco's committee work and Ume's customer-facing/dramatic roles become more definite, but no complete work audit or equal contribution follows. Ume's persona management and theatrical boasting produce obligations of their own rather than simply releasing her from work.

**CL-B0247-091 — The final wait preserves a band relationship after the formal groups disperse.**

The school tasks do not end in unanimous praise: Ume claims talent, Hina reports moving acting and Emoco recalls laughter and a sleep-like interruption. Ume's denial prevents the disputed pause from becoming an unqualified factual sleep verdict, even though the comedy strongly invites that inference. Hina's report of committee welcome contradicts Emoco's stated expectation of being unwelcome; Emoco's own account emphasizes others' paper-filing work under her look. When she finally asks why the other two waited, Ume names compensation and Hina names a party for the three members of 4U. Waiting is an enacted availability; its mixed explanations and familiar food quarrel preserve friction rather than dissolving it into a final harmonious cure.

## Comparison without an invented common chronology

**CL-B0247-092 — Shared work can change a judgment without making a permanent identity.**

Futaba's revision of a two-person stage ideal, Ferb's particular appreciation of Yumeno's sound and the return to a three-person 4U occasion each place a relationship in a new local arrangement. None automatically creates a new enduring unit or erases earlier particular boundaries. The twins' recognition, Ferb's clarification and 4U's waiting have different mechanisms and degrees of explicitness; comparison concerns situated cooperation, not a single chronology or common psychological cure.

**CL-B0247-095 — An ending proposal carries less completion than an enacted or explicitly reported act.**
